Let's propose an integrated algorithm:

Algorithm summary for final docstring:
- Use scale-invariant internal score.
- Initial candidates: inherited sequence, uniform, sine/cosine templates of several phases/amplitudes, low-dimensional exponential Fourier+polynomial coordinate search.
- Full-dimensional local search on the simplex using a mirror-descent/probabilistic update based on a soft active set of the largest convolution coefficients. Use adaptive step, low-pass smoothing, backtracking, occasional perturbations and length-upsampling.
- Validate few candidates with `evaluate_sequence` and return best.

Need implement top-level helpers, no closures, no lambdas. Global state variables maybe.

Let's detail functions:

`_next_pow2(m)` as before.

`_fft_convolve(a)`: return full conv.

`_score_from_c(a, c)`: sum = a.sum(); if sum<0.01 inf; return 2*n*max(c)/sum^2. Use normalized p sum=1 to avoid recompute.

`_internal_score_and_c(a)`: sanitize, clip, normalize? For search we want sequence normalized to sum=1. Return (p normalized, c, score). But for candidate fallback, fit. For objective, use normalized. We can have `_prepare(a)` that clips nonfinite, nonnegative, if sum<0.01 use uniform, normalize. Return p.

`_convolve_normalized(p)`: assumes sum ~1.

`_active_gradient(p, c, K, beta)`: compute weights and grad.
Implementation details:
```
def _active_gradient(p, c, K, beta):
    n=len(p)
    if K >= c.size: idx=np.arange(c.size); vals=c; 
    else:
       idx=np.argpartition(c, -K)[-K:]
       vals=c[idx]
    cmax=vals.max()
    # If vals flat, uniform; else softmax
    if beta <=0 or cmax <=0:
       w=np.ones(K)/K
    else:
       z=(vals-cmax)*beta
       # z<=0
       w=np.exp(z); s=w.sum(); if s==0 or not finite: w=ones; else w/=s
    grad=np.zeros(n)
    for kk,ww in zip(idx,w):
       if ww <= 1e-15: continue
       k=int(kk)
       l=k-(n-1); if l<0: l=0
       r=k; if r>=n: r=n-1
       if r<l: continue
       # p indices k-r down to k-l
       seg = p[k-r:k-l+1][::-1]
       grad[l:r+1] += (2.0*ww)*seg
    mean = grad.mean()
    grad -= mean
    # normalize by n? optional
    return grad
```
Weighted K: use K=16+ maybe. We can choose K adapt based on n: K = max(8, min(40, n//128? 16)). For n=2048 K=16. For 8192 K=32.

`_lowpass_center(g, cutoff)`:
```
def _lowpass_center(g, cutoff):
    n=len(g)
    if cutoff <=0: return g.copy()
    G=np.fft.rfft(g)
    keep=min(len(G), cutoff+1)
    if keep < len(G): G[keep:]=0
    out=np.fft.irfft(G, n=n)
    out -= out.mean()
    return out
```
If cutoff = n//2 no effect (with rfft keep all). We'll use cutoff maybe 0 no smooth, or 1/8n.

q = np.maximum(q, 0.0)
# add small floor?
ts = q.sum()
if not finite or ts<0.01: return p
q /= ts
q = np.minimum(q, 1000.0)
# renormalize after clip? if clipped maybe sum changes; q/=sum
s2=q.sum(); if s2>0: q/=s2
return q
```

def _project_simplex(v):
    v=np.asarray(v,float); n=len(v); mu=0; r=np.sort(v)[::-1]; cum=np.cumsum(r); cond = r - (cum+1)/np.arange(1,n+1)
    # find last positive? standard
    idx=np.where(cond>0)[0]
    if len(idx)==0: mu=(v.sum()+1)/n? Actually projection onto sum=1: the only point all 1/n? Standard formula for projection onto {x>=0, sum=1}: maximize <v,x> -0.5||x||^2 subject x>=0 sum=1. Let x_i = max(v_i - μ,0), choose μ such that sum=1.
    # Use binary search for μ if n small? Or vectorized.
```
Simpler binary search μ:
- Objective sum max(v_i - μ,0)=1. μ range [min(v)-1, max(v)]. Binary search 50 iterations. n=4096, 50*4096=200k per projection, maybe heavy but okay for occasional.
Vectorized standard:
```
def _project_simplex(v):
    n=len(v)
    u=np.sort(v)[::-1]
    css=np.cumsum(u)
    rho = np.nonzero(u - css/np.arange(1,n+1) > 0)[0]
    if len(rho)==0:
        return np.full(n, 1.0/n)
    rho = rho[-1]
    theta = (css[rho] - 1) / (rho + 1)
    return np.maximum(v - theta, 0.0)
```
This is O(n logn) sort. Maybe okay for occasional.
Additive update: `d = -grad` centered; for step α, q=p+α*d; q_project = project_simplex(q); normalize (projection ensures sum1). Evaluate. Could be used as alternate update direction; maybe better for zeroing. We can choose update mode each block: mirror for safety, additive for exact sum. Backtracking with projection.

def _local_search(p0, budget=-1, n_preset=None, tag='', lowpass_phases=True):
    p = _prepare(p0)
    N = len(p)
    c = _fft_convolve(p); score=...
    best = p.copy(); best_score=score
    lr=0.05
    K=12
    beta=30
    smooth_cutoff=0
    it=0; no_improve=0; next_perturb=100
    while time < budget:
       # adapt K and cutoff based on progress
       if it < 500: K=12; beta=20; cutoff=0
       elif it < 2000: K=24; beta=40; cutoff=min(16, N//64)
       else: K=16; beta=80; cutoff=0 (fine)
       # maybe alternate smoothed/raw
       grad = _active_gradient(p,c,K,beta)
       if smooth_cutoff>0 and it%2==0: grad = _lowpass_center(grad, smooth_cutoff)
       if not any: break
       q = _mirror_update(p, grad, lr)
       if np.array_equal(q,p): use additive
       c_q = _fft_convolve(q); score_q = ...
       if score_q < score - 1e-12:
          p=q; c=c_q; score=score_q; lr=min(1.0, lr*1.1)
          if score < best_score: best=p.copy(); best_score=score; no_improve=0
          else no_improve+=1
       else:
          lr = max(0.001, lr*0.8)
          no_improve+=1
          if no_improve > 50:
             # random perturbation of best
             p=best.copy()
             noise = rng.normal(size=N)*0.05
             p = p*np.exp(noise); p/=p.sum()
             c=conv; score=...
             # if too worse, reset to best
             if score > best_score*1.2: p=best.copy(); c=...; score=best_score
             lr=0.05; no_improve=0
       it += 1
       if it % 200 ==0: maybe try larger length? not here
    return best,best_score
```

Upsampling function:
```
def _upsample_seq(p, n_new):
    n_old=len(p)
    if n_new==n_old: return p.copy()
    if n_new > n_old:
       # linear interpolation on index centers
       x_old=np.linspace(0,1,n_old)
       x_new=np.linspace(0,1,n_new)
       q=np.interp(x_new, x_old, p)
       q=np.maximum(q,0); normalize
    else: # downsample by averaging blocks or interpolation (could lose)
       # use interpolation to n_new
```

def _low_dim_optimize(budget_s, seed=1, n=1024, D=None, max_step=1.0):
    bases = _make_bases(n)
    D = bases.shape[0]
    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)

    # maybe set some initial better: theta for sin1? Let's let random.

    score = _internal_score(seq)
    best_theta=theta.copy(); best_seq=seq.copy(); best_score=score
    steps = np.full(D,0.3)
    no_improve_coords = np.zeros(D)
    pass_no=0
    start=time.monotonic()

       pass_no +=1

Upsampling/downsampling helper:
```
def _resample_seq(p, n_new):
    p=_prepare(p)
    n_old=len(p)
    if n_new==n_old: return p.copy()
    x_old=np.linspace(0.0,1.0,n_old)
    x_new=np.linspace(0.0,1.0,n_new)
    q=np.interp(x_new,x_old,p)
    q=np.where(finite,q,0); q=np.maximum(q,0)
    # integral approx, normalize
    s=q.sum(); if s<1e-12: q=np.full(n_new,1/n_new); else q/=s
    return q
```
If n_new < n_old and want preserve shape, interp okay.

Let's design `_local_search` with time-based phases and more robust:
- Use a small number of alternative update profiles keyed by `profile`:
   profile 0: smooth broad (large K, lowpass)
   profile 1: active sharp (small K, high beta, no lowpass)
   profile 2: additive/projected (for escaping)
We can cycle or use based on no_improve.

Pseudo:
```
def _local_search(p0, budget, seed=0, min_budget=10):
   p=_prepare(p0); best=p.copy(); best_score=score
   n=len(p); t_end=...; it=0; lr=0.05; no=0
   K= max(8, min(32, n//128))
   beta=30
   cutoff=0
   while time < t_end:
       # adapt based on it or elapsed
       frac = elapsed/budget
       if frac <0.2: K=12; beta=20; cutoff=max(0, min(n//2, 4)) maybe no
       elif frac<0.5: K=24; beta=50; cutoff=n//256
       elif frac<0.8: K=16; beta=100; cutoff=0
       else: K=32; beta=200; cutoff=n//128? fine
       # every 500 iters, randomize K? maybe.
       grad = _active_gradient(p,c,K,beta)
       if cutoff>0 and (it%2==0): grad = _lowpass_center(grad,cutoff)
       q=_mirror_update(p,grad,lr)
       # if q identical, try additive
       c2=_fft_convolve(q); sc2=...
       if sc2 < score:
           p=q; c=c2; score=sc2; lr=min(1.0, lr*1.05); no=0
           if sc2 < best_score: best=p.copy(); best_score=sc2
       else:
           # try half step if lr>min
           lr2=lr*0.5
           if lr2>min:
              q2=_mirror_update(p,grad,lr2); c2b=...; scb=...
              if scb < score:
                  p=q2; ...; lr=lr2; no=0; best update
               else:
                  lr=lr2; no+=1
           else:
                # additive step
                d = -grad*n; for alpha [lr,lr*0.5,...]
                projected; evaluate accept
                no+=1
       if no>200:
           # start from best with random multiplicative perturbation
           p=best.copy(); c=...; score=best_score
           noise = rng.normal(size=n)*0.05
           p = p*np.exp(np.clip(noise,-2,2)); normalize
           c=conv; score=...
           # if perturbed not too worse allow as current, else best
           if score > best_score*1.05:
               p=best.copy(); c=...; score=best_score
           lr=0.03; no=0
       it +=1
   return best,best_score
```
Need c for best. We can recompute occasionally.

Let's implement recurrence template:
```
def _recurrence_template(n, target='flat', shift=0, q=None):
   L=2*n-1
   p=np.zeros(n)
   if q is None:
      q = np.full(n, 1.0/L)
   q0=q[0]
   if q0 <=0: return uniform
   p[0]=np.sqrt(q0)
   for k in range(1,n):
       if k-1 >=1:
           # dot p[1:k] with reversed p[1:k]
           left = p[1:k]
           right = p[k-1:0:-1]  # careful if k-1=0
           s = np.dot(left, right)
       else: s=0.0
       denom=2*p[0]
       val=(q[k]-s)/denom
       if not finite or val<0: val=1e-12? or max(0,val)
       p[k]=val
   # maybe target q can be tapered: q_k = base * (1 + taper?) We can variants.
   if shift>0: np.roll? shift right with zeros: out=np.zeros(n); out[shift:]=p[:n-shift]; p=out
   p = np.maximum(p,0); normalize, floor.
   return p
```
Dot arrays: `p[k-1:0:-1]` when k=2 -> p[1:0:-1] = p[1]? yes length1. k=1 -> p[0:0:-1] empty. Good.
If val negative, clipping may make future s lower and p maybe positive. But if many negative, template poor. We can return if p[k] negative significant? Maybe use `val = max(0.0, val)`.

- flat: 1/L.
- ramp down upper? We only set first n. To balance upper, perhaps set q0..q_{n-1} not all equal; increasing toward center? Let's think: Recurrence target first half. The actual upper max likely around n. To reduce upper, maybe lower target near n? But recurrence only controls first n; if target q_{n-1}=M, normalized first half M/S^2. Upper coefficients depend on p. Hard. We can generate a few target profiles:
   `q_k = (1/L) * (1 + torque * k/(n-1))` maybe lower/upper. But sum S changes. We can just add flat and maybe decreasing (q0 higher?) Let's not overdo.

Maybe use a modified recurrence targeting all 2n-1 by alternating: There are n unknowns, 2n-1 equations. We can choose q_k as unknown to minimize max? Not.

Detailed `_low_dim_search` implementation:
```
def _low_dim_search(n, budget_s, seed=1, max_mode=10, template_seqs=None):
    if budget_s <= 5: return None
    bases = _make_bases(n, max_mode=max_mode)
    D = bases.shape[0]
    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)
    candidates_theta=[]
    if template_seqs:
       for seq in template_seqs:
           p=_prepare_sequence(seq, add_floor=False)
           if p is None: continue
           theta=_theta_from_sequence(p,bases)
           if theta is not None: candidates_theta.append(theta)
    if not candidates_theta:
       candidates_theta=[np.zeros(D)]
       for _ in range(3): candidates_theta.append(rng.normal(0,0.3,D))
    # Limit number of starts
    best_p=None; best_score=np.inf
    start=time.monotonic()
    # If many starts, allocate per start
    time_per_start = min(30.0, budget_s/(len(candidates_theta)+1))
    for idx, th in enumerate(candidates_theta):
       if time.monotonic() - start >= budget_s - 30: break
       # coordinate search from theta until time_per_start or no improve
       theta=th.copy()
       p=_sequence_from_theta(theta,bases)
       score=_internal_score(p)
       local_best_theta=theta.copy(); local_best_score=score; local_best_p=p.copy()
       steps=np.full(D,0.25)
       stagnant=0
       # Set a sub-deadline
       sub_deadline = time.monotonic() + time_per_start
       coord_pass=0
       while sub_deadline - time.monotonic() > 1.0 and stagnant < D*3:
           coord_pass +=1
           improved=False
           for j in range(D):
               before_score=score
               # evaluate + and -
               old=theta[j]
               best_val=before_score; best_angle=0.0
               for sign in (1.0,-1.0):
                   theta[j]=old + sign*steps[j]
                   z = bases.dot(theta) ...
                   p2=_sequence_from_theta(theta,bases); sc2=_internal_score(p2)
                   if sc2 < best_val:
                       best_val=sc2; best_angle=theta[j]
               theta[j]=best_angle
               p=_sequence_from_theta(theta,bases) if best_angle != old? Could reuse eval if best_angle old? We need p. Simpler recompute.
               score=best_val
               if score < before_score - 1e-12:
                   improved=True; steps[j]=min(1.0, steps[j]*1.2)
                   if score < local_best_score:
                       local_best_score=score; local_best_p=p.copy(); local_best_theta=theta.copy()
               else:
                   steps[j]=max(1e-4, steps[j]*0.85)
           if improved:
               stagnant=0
           else:
               stagnant += D
               # random kick
               theta = local_best_theta.copy() + rng.normal(0,0.15,D)
               p=_sequence_from_theta(theta,bases); score=_internal_score(p)
               if score > local_best_score*1.1:
                   theta=local_best_theta.copy(); p=local_best_p.copy(); score=local_best_score
       # update global
       if local_best_score < best_score:
           best_score=local_best_score; best_p=local_best_p.copy()
    # Optional final coordinate from global best theta for remaining if any
    if best_p is not None and time.monotonic() - start < budget_s - 5:
       # We don't have global best theta unless stored. Could store best_theta.
```
Need store best_theta when update. But final not necessary.
This coordinate search recomputes `p` multiple times; okay but can optimize by caching when best_angle old.

However, `_internal_score(p)` inside coordinate uses p not normalized? It normalizes, clips. Each time cost. Could optimize by computing from f sum but okay.

z = np.dot(bases, theta)
z = np.clip(np.where(np.isfinite(z), z, 0.0), -5.0,5.0)
f = np.exp(z)
f = np.where(f>0, f, 1e-300)
s=f.sum(); if not finite/s<1e-12: f=np.full(n,1.0/n)
else f/=s
return f
```

except: theta=None
if theta is not None: clip theta to [-3,3]
```

Now, `_make_template_sine` to use for low-dim and candidate. Implement:
```
def _make_template_sine(n, amp, phase=0.0, mode=1, zero_boundary=False):
   n=int(n)
   if n<=0: return None
   x=(np.arange(n)+0.5)/float(n)
   ang=2*np.pi*mode*x + phase
   y=1.0 + amp*np.sin(ang)  # or cos if phase=pi/2?
   # To include cos, can use phase; sin with phase covers.
   y=np.maximum(y, 1e-6)
   y/=y.sum(); return y
```
For negative amp with endpoint? fine.

Maybe use a "smooth bump" template: y=1+amp*np.sin(np.pi*x) (symmetric, sum positive) or y=1+amp*np.cos(2πx) phases. We'll include phase so sine covers.

Full local search: Need `_internal_score` returns score maybe uses `_fft_convolve`. We can combine score and conv to avoid double:
```
def _score_and_conv(p):
    p=_prepare_sequence(p, add_floor=False)
    c=_fft_convolve(p)
    s=float(p.sum())
    if s<0.01: return p,c,np.inf
    score=2.0*len(p)*float(np.max(c))/(s*s)
    return p,c,score
```
But in local search p normalized sum1; use `_score_from_conv` to avoid sum. For candidate initial, use `_score_and_conv`.

`_mirror_update` can use p normalized. It should ensure sum1, values finite. Use `np.exp(exponent, out=...)` to reduce allocation? Not necessary.
```
def _mirror_update(p, grad, lr):
   n=p.size
   g = grad - grad.mean()  # ensure center
   g = g * n
   ex = -float(lr) * g
   np.clip(ex, -5.0, 5.0, out=ex)
   q = p * np.exp(ex)
   q = np.where(np.isfinite(q), q, 0.0)
   q = np.maximum(q, 0.0)
   # floor small: np.maximum(q, 1e-300) maybe
   s=q.sum()
   if not finite or s<=0: return p.copy()
   q /= s
   return q
```
If all p positive, fine. If grad has NaN due c? sanitize.

`_active_gradient` returns centered grad. If c has NaN, return zeros. Use `np.isfinite`.

`_current_time()` just use time.monotonic.

`_next_pow2(m)`:
```
def _next_pow2(m):
    n=1
    while n < m:
        n <<=1
    return n
```

`_fft_convolve(a)`:
```
def _fft_convolve(a):
    a=np.asarray(a,dtype=np.float64)
    n=a.size
    if n==0: return np.empty(0)
    need=2*n-1
    N=_next_pow2(need)
    fa=np.fft.rfft(a,N)
    c=np.fft.irfft(fa*fa,N)
    return c[:need]
```
For n=1 need1 N1; works.

`_prepare_sequence(x, add_floor=True)`:
```
def _prepare_sequence(x, add_floor=True):
    if x is None: return None
    try:
       arr=np.asarray(x,dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    except: return None
    if arr.size==0: return None
    arr=np.where(np.isfinite(arr), arr, 0.0)
    arr=np.clip(arr,0.0,1000.0)
    s=arr.sum()
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s<0.01:
       return np.full(arr.size, 1.0/arr.size)
    arr=arr/s
    if add_floor:
       floor=1e-12/arr.size
       arr=np.maximum(arr, floor)
       s=arr.sum(); if s>0: arr/=s
    return arr
```
For n large floor tiny. For uniform if size=0 no.

`_score_from_conv(n, c, sum_a=1.0)`:
```
def _score_from_conv(n,c,sum_a=1.0):
    if c.size==0: return np.inf
    mx=float(np.max(c))
    if not np.isfinite(mx): return np.inf
    if mx<0: mx=0.0
    s=float(sum_a)
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s<0.01: return np.inf
    return 2.0*n*mx/(s*s)
```

`_score_and_conv(x)` returns p,c,score:
```
def _score_and_conv(x):
    p=_prepare_sequence(x, add_floor=False)
    if p is None: return None,None,np.inf
    c=_fft_convolve(p)
    score=_score_from_conv(len(p), c, float(p.sum()))
    return p,c,score
```

`_internal_score(x)` just `_score_and_conv(x)[2]`.

`_upsample_seq(p, n_new)`:
```
def _upsample_seq(p, n_new):
    p=_prepare_sequence(p, add_floor=False)
    n_new=int(n_new)
    if p is None or n_new<=0: return None
    if n_new==len(p): return p.copy()
    old=np.arange(p.size, dtype=np.float64)
    new=(np.arange(n_new, dtype=np.float64) + 0.5)
    # map to old interval? Use x = (i+0.5)/n to centers.
    x_new=(np.arange(n_new)+0.5)/n_new
    x_old=(np.arange(p.size)+0.5)/p.size
    q=np.interp(x_new, x_old, p)
    q=np.where(np.isfinite(q),q,0.0); q=np.maximum(q,0.0)
    s=q.sum(); if s<1e-12: q=np.full(n_new,1.0/n_new); else q/=s
    return q
```
Maybe for downsampling many blocks, interpolation okay. For upsample from 1024 to 2048, centers align.

`_make_template_sine(n, amp, phase=0.0, mode=1)`:
```
x=(np.arange(n)+0.5)/float(n)
ang=2π*mode*x + phase
y=1+amp*np.sin(ang)
y=np.maximum(y, 1e-6)
s=y.sum(); if s<1e-12: return uniform
return y/s
```
If amp large >1, clipping. Fine.

`_recurrence_template(n, shift=0, taper=0.0)`:
Implement target q first n. If taper: q[k] = base*(1 + taper*k/(n-1)), then normalize? The target q should be a sequence of coefficients for unren; scaling changes S and normalized lower all scale same if q scaled, normalized unaffected? If q scaled by constant, p scaled? Recurrence p0 sqrt(q0) scales sqrt, p linear? Overall p? If q all scaled by C, p scales sqrt? Let's check: p0√C; p_k linear in q and product => p_k scales C? Wait p1=(Cq - C p1^2? If p1 scales C? Product p_i p_k scales C^2, inconsistent. So scaling target not homogeneous. So target absolute matters. Need choose q values ~1/L. Taper relative may not be simple. Skip taper or use q with different constant average preserving sum? The normalized result depends. Keep flat.
```
def _recurrence_template(n, shift=0):
    n=int(n)
    if n<=0: return None
    L=2*n-1
    q=None
    p=np.zeros(n)
    p[0]=1.0/np.sqrt(L)
    for k in range(1,n):
       if k>1:
          a=p[1:k]
          b=p[k-1:0:-1]
          s=float(np.dot(a,b))
       else: s=0.0
       denom=2.0*p[0]
       val=(1.0/L - s)/denom
       if not np.isfinite(val) or val < 0.0:
          val=0.0
       p[k]=val
    if shift>0:
       out=np.zeros(n)
       shift=min(int(shift),n-1)
       if n-shift>0: out[shift:]=p[:n-shift]
       p=out
    p=_prepare_sequence(p, add_floor=True)
    return p
```
`_prepare_sequence` normalizes, floor. If shift sets first zeros, floor makes positive tiny. Good.

Maybe recurrence p sum may be >1000? No after normalize sum1, max maybe p0_norm ~? If p0 .015, sum maybe? If sum large 6, normalized p0 ~.0026; max okay. Values <=1. Good.

`_make_bases(n, max_mode=10)`:
```
def _make_bases(n, max_mode=10):
    n=int(n); if n<=1: return np.zeros((1,n))
    x=(np.arange(n)+0.5)/float(n)
    parts=[]
    m=1
    while m <= max_mode:
       ang=2*np.pi*m*x
       parts.append(np.sin(ang))
       parts.append(np.cos(ang))
       m+=1
    xm=x-0.5
    parts.append(xm)
    parts.append(xm*xm - 1.0/12.0)
    parts.append(xm*xm*xm)
    B=np.vstack(parts)
    # Normalize columns? Maybe standardize each basis to unit norm to improve coordinate steps.
    for j in range(B.shape[0]):
       norm=np.linalg.norm(B[j])
       if norm>1e-8: B[j] /= norm/np.sqrt(n)? Wait if we use steps in theta, bases scaled affects step. We want theta step ~0.1 gives additive z amplitude ~? If basis unit RMS, z amplitude = theta * rms. If include n, maybe. Let's scale to RMS 1: B[j] /= np.sqrt(np.mean(B[j]**2)). Then theta=0.1 gives O(0.1). Simpler: just leave sin amplitude ~1; linear amplitude .5. Step .25 okay. But if include norm, coordinate symmetric.
    return B
```
We can standardize each basis to have zero mean and std 1:
```
row = row - row.mean(); std=np.sqrt(np.mean(row*row)+1e-12); row/=std; parts.append(row)
```
For sin/cos, std ~0.707 -> scaled up. Then theta step 0.2 => z amplitude 0.2. Good. Let's do.

`_sequence_from_theta(theta, bases)`:
```
def _sequence_from_theta(theta, bases):
    z=np.dot(bases, theta)
    z=np.where(np.isfinite(z),z,0.0)
    z=np.clip(z,-5.0,5.0)
    f=np.exp(z)
    f=np.where(f>0,f,1e-300)
    s=f.sum()
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s<1e-12:
       n=bases.shape[1]; return np.full(n,1.0/n)
    f/=s
    return f
```

`_theta_from_sequence(seq, bases)`:
```
def _theta_from_sequence(seq, bases):
    p=_prepare_sequence(seq, add_floor=False)
    if p is None: return None
    y=np.log(np.maximum(p,1e-12))
    y-=y.mean()
    try:
       theta, _, _, _ = np.linalg.lstsq(bases, y, rcond=None)
    except Exception:
       return None
    theta=np.where(np.isfinite(theta),theta,0.0)
    theta=np.clip(theta,-4.0,4.0)
    return theta
```

def _low_dim_search(n, budget_s, seed=1, template_seqs=None, max_mode=10):
    n=int(n); if n<=0 or budget_s <= 5: return None, np.inf

    if not starts:

Let's design a top-level `_coordinate_search(theta, bases, deadline, seed, rng?)`? `rng` object cannot be passed? yes.
```
def _coordinate_search(theta, bases, deadline, initial_score=None, seed=0):
    rng=np.random.default_rng(seed)
    D=len(theta); theta=theta.copy()
    p=_sequence_from_theta(theta,bases)
    if initial_score is None: score=_internal_score(p)
    else: score=initial_score
    best_theta=theta.copy(); best_p=p.copy(); best_score=score
    steps=np.full(D,0.25)
    stagnant=0
    while time.monotonic() < deadline:
       improved=False
       for j in range(D):
           if time.monotonic() >= deadline: break
           old=theta[j]
           best_val=score; best_ang=old
           theta[j]=old+steps[j]; p2=...; sc2=...
           if sc2<best_val: best_val=sc2; best_ang=theta[j]
           theta[j]=old-steps[j]; p3=...; sc3=...
           if sc3<best_val: best_val=sc3; best_ang=theta[j]
           theta[j]=best_ang
           if best_ang != old:
               # recompute p and score for consistency (maybe p2 or p3 already)
               p=_sequence_from_theta(theta,bases)
               score=best_val
               improved=True
               steps[j]=min(1.0, steps[j]*1.2)
               if score<best_score:
                   best_score=score; best_p=p.copy(); best_theta=theta.copy()
           else:
               steps[j]=max(1e-4, steps[j]*0.8)
       stag=not improved
       if stag:
          stagnation +=1
          if stagnation>=4:
             # kick
             theta=best_theta.copy()+rng.normal(0,0.1,D)
             p=_sequence_from_theta(theta,bases); score=_internal_score(p)
             stagnation=0
             # if too bad reset
             if score>best_score*1.1:
                theta=best_theta.copy(); p=best_p.copy(); score=best_score
          steps *= 0.98
       else:
          stagnation=0
    return best_p,best_score,best_theta
```
Then `_low_dim_search` creates starts, for each calls `_coordinate_search` with sub-deadline. Finally after starts, if time remains, calls `_coordinate_search` from best_theta with remaining (need store best_theta from coordinate). Good.
Need no lambdas; fine.

But note: In `_coordinate_search`, if best_ang old, `score` remains old, p maybe from last coordinate changed? We set theta to best_ang (old); p unchanged, score unchanged. Good.
If improvement in plus then minus not, we evaluate minus after plus but leave theta at minus; then set to best_ang old. Good.

Step sizes: Standardize bases to RMS1. Step initial 0.25. Max step 1.5? Maybe 1.0 too small for exp amplitude? z amplitude 1; e factor 2.7. Good. If coordinate improves, grow to max 2.0? Maybe max 2.

Base standardization: For each row, zero mean, scale to standard deviation 1.
```
row=row-row.mean(); std=np.sqrt(np.mean(row*row)); if std>1e-12: row/=std
```
Thus theta ~0.25 -> z amplitude .25. Good.

In `_theta_from_sequence`, solving lstsq with standardized bases works.

`_local_search(p0, budget_s, seed=0, tag='')`:
```
def _local_search(p0, budget_s, seed=0, tag='local'):
    p=_prepare_sequence(p0, add_floor=True)
    if p is None: return None, np.inf
    n=p.size
    c=_fft_convolve(p)
    score=_score_from_conv(n,c,1.0)
    best=p.copy(); best_score=score
    rng=np.random.default_rng(seed)
    t0=time.monotonic(); t_end=t0+max(0.0,budget_s-5.0)
    lr=0.05
    no_improve=0
    it=0
    base_K = max(8, min(32, n//128 + 8))  # for 2048:24? n//128=16+8=24 (maybe high); perhaps n//256+8: 2048/256=8+8=16, 4096=16+8=24, 8192=32+8=40 cap32. good.
    while time.monotonic() < t_end:
        frac = (time.monotonic()-t0)/max(1e-9,budget_s)
        if frac < 0.25:
            K=base_K; beta=20.0; cutoff=0
        elif frac < 0.5:
            K=min(64, 2*base_K); beta=8.0; cutoff=0
        elif frac < 0.75:
            K=base_K; beta=80.0; cutoff=max(1, min(64, n//512))
        else:
            K=max(4, base_K//2); beta=300.0; cutoff=0
        # every few hundred iters, alternate smoothed
        if cutoff > 0 and (it % 2 == 1): use_cutoff = cutoff else 0 maybe.
        grad = _active_gradient(p,c,K,beta)
        if not np.all(np.isfinite(grad)):
             grad=np.zeros(n); break? try reset.
        g_use = grad
        if cutoff > 0 and it % 3 == 0:
             g_use = _lowpass_center(grad, cutoff)
        q=_mirror_update(p,g_use,lr)
        c2=_fft_convolve(q)
        sc2=_score_from_conv(n,c2,1.0)
        accepted=False
        if np.isfinite(sc2) and sc2 < score:
            p=q; c=c2; score=sc2; lr=min(1.5, lr*1.07); accepted=True
            if sc2 < best_score:
                best=p.copy(); best_score=sc2; no_improve=0
            else: no_improve+=1
        else:
            # try half
            if lr > 0.002:
                q2=_mirror_update(p,g_use,lr*0.5)
                c2b=_fft_convolve(q2)
                scb=_score_from_conv(n,c2b,1.0)
                if np.isfinite(scb) and scb < score:
                    p=q2; c=c2b; score=scb; lr *=0.5; accepted=True
                    if scb<best_score: best=p.copy(); best_score=scb; no_improve=0
                    else: no_improve+=1
                else:
                    lr=max(0.001, lr*0.8); no_improve+=1
            else:
                lr=0.01; no_improve+=1
        if no_improve > 120:
            # reset plus perturb best
            p=best.copy()
            c=_fft_convolve(p); score=_score_from_conv(n,c,1.0)
            if budget allows:
                temp=0.05
                noise=rng.normal(0.0,temp,n)
                noise=np.clip(noise,-3,3)
                pp=p*np.exp(noise); s=pp.sum(); if s>0: pp/=s
                cp=_fft_convolve(pp); scp=_score_from_conv(n,cp,1.0)
                if np.isfinite(scp):
                    p=pp; c=cp; score=scp
                    if score > best_score*1.1:
                        p=best.copy(); c=_fft_convolve(p); score=best_score
            lr=0.04; no_improve=0
        it += 1
        if it % 200 == 0:
            print tag it best
    return best, best_score
```
Potential inefficiency: On reject, we compute two trial convs (full and half). On accept one. Average maybe 1.5. Good.

But if fc<1? `budget_s` from remaining maybe 0; handle.

Active gradient with K maybe > c.size? For n small. In `_active_gradient`, if K>len(c), K=len. Use argpartition if K < len, else all. For K large (64) c size 4095, okay.

Beta softmax when vals all near max: if cmax = max vals, z<=0. Use `z = beta*(vals - cmax)`. If beta*diff underflows all exp to 0? exp of negative <=1; if beta diff very negative, some zeros, at least max z=0 gives exp1. Sum >0. Good.

But `vals` from top K; if K=1, gradient for one lag. Fine.

`_lowpass_center`: if cutoff limited. For n maybe 1024, n//512=2. Use rfft length n//2+1; keep cutoff+1. If cutoff >= len(G), no change. Implement:
```
def _lowpass_center(g, cutoff):
    n=g.size
    if cutoff <= 1: return g.copy()
    G=np.fft.rfft(g)
    keep=min(len(G), int(cutoff)+1)
    if keep < len(G): G[keep:]=0.0
    out=np.fft.irfft(G,n=n)
    out -= out.mean()
    return out
```

`_mirror_update`: if grad all zeros, q=p (score same). Avoid; maybe if grad max tiny, return p. Add check:
```
if not np.any(np.abs(grad)>1e-18): return p.copy()
```

Need ensure p sum exactly 1 after update; `s=q.sum(); q/=s`. If q has inf/nan, fallback.

    return [(name,p,score) for score,i,name,p in arr]

Compute θ_grad:
```
def _theta_gradient(p, bases, center_grad):
    # center_grad already centered in p coords
    # We need sum p_i * g_i * B_j and m_j = sum p_i B_j
    B = bases  # D x n
    # g_weight = center_grad * p
    wg = center_grad * p
    # term1_j = sum_i wg_i B_ji = B @ wg
    term1 = B.dot(wg)
    m = B.dot(p)
    term2 = m * wg.sum()  # wg.sum() maybe ~0 because center_grad centered but p weight not exactly? Actually ∑p_i center_grad_i not zero generally (center_grad mean zero, weighted sum not zero). But derivative formula: ∂p_i/∂θ = p_i(B_ji - m_j). Dot g =∑ g_i p_i B_ji - m_j ∑ g_i p_i. Correct.
    return term1 - m * (wg.sum())
```
Wait `center_grad` is gradient wrt p of M? It was centered by subtracting mean, not weighted mean. The true gradient of M with no sum constraint is raw g. On tangent sum-zero directions, any vector g + λ1 has same dot. We can choose g_centered_mean (arithmetic) because dp/dθ sum zero: ∑ dp/dθ_i =0, so dot with arith-centered = dot with raw. Good. For θ_grad formula with centered g, the normalization term uses ∑ g_i p_i? Actually derivative formula already includes normalization; using centered g:
h_j = ∑_i c_i p_i (B_ji - m_j), where c = centered raw grad. This is valid. `wg = c * p`.

Adam:
```
m1 = zeros; v1=zeros; t=1; step=0.1
while time:
  c=conv(p)
  gradp = _active_gradient(p,c,K,beta)  # centered
  thgrad = _theta_gradient(p,bases,gradp)
  # normalize / clip
  thgrad = np.where(finite, thgrad,0)
  m1=0.9*m1+0.1*thgrad; v1=0.99*v1+0.01*thgrad^2
  update = m1/(np.sqrt(v1)+1e-8)
  # clip update norm to max_update maybe 2
  norm=np.linalg.norm(update); if norm>1: update/=norm
  for step_trial in [step, step*0.5, step*2?]
      th_new=theta - step_trial*update
      p_trial=seq; sc=score
      if sc<score: accept; t +=1; step=... 
  if no improve, step*0.7; if no improve many, random kick
```

`_low_dim_grad_search` pseudo:
```
def _low_dim_grad_search(theta, bases, deadline, seed=0, p=None, score=np.inf):
    theta=theta.copy()
    if p is None: p=_sequence_from_theta(theta,bases)
    if not finite score: score=_internal_score(p)
    best_theta=theta.copy(); best_p=p.copy(); best_score=score
    m1=np.zeros(D); v1=np.zeros(D); step=0.1; no_improve=0
    rng=np.random.default_rng(seed)
    it=0
    K = 16; beta=30
    while time.monotonic()<deadline:
        # get c and gradp
        c=_fft_convolve(p)
        gradp=_active_gradient(p,c,K,beta)
        thgrad=_theta_gradient(p,bases,gradp)
        # if thgrad tiny, break/kick
        m1=0.9*m1+0.1*thgrad
        v1=0.99*v1+0.01*thgrad*thgrad
        update=m1/(np.sqrt(v1)+1e-8)
        nrm=np.linalg.norm(update)
        if not finite(nrm) or nrm<1e-12:
            # kick
            step*=0.5; no_improve+=1
            if no_improve>50: break
            continue
        if nrm>1.0: update=update/nrm*1.0
        accepted=False
        for trial_step in (step, step*0.5, step*2.0):
            th=theta - trial_step*update
            th=np.clip(th,-6,6)
            pt=_sequence_from_theta(th,bases)
            ct=_fft_convolve(pt)
            sct=_score_from_conv(len(pt),ct,1.0)
            if np.isfinite(sct) and sct < score:
                theta=th; p=pt; c=ct; score=sct; accepted=True
                if sct < best_score: best...; no_improve=0
                break
        if accepted:
            step=min(0.5, step*1.1)
        else:
            step=max(0.01, step*0.7)
            no_improve+=1
            if no_improve>60:
                # kick from best
                theta=best_theta.copy()+rng.normal(0,0.2,D)
                p=_sequence_from_theta(theta,bases); c=...; score=...
                no_improve=0; step=0.1
        it+=1
    return best_theta,best_score,best_p
```
This uses gradient ascent? We minimize, so theta - step*grad. Good.
K/beta can adapt by it/fraction: early K=12, beta=20; later K=24, beta=80. Simpler fixed K=20 beta=40. For low-dim small n, okay.

Then `_low_dim_search` can generate start thetas and call `_low_dim_grad_search`, instead of coordinate. That is more continuous and likely better. We can also include final coordinate from best? Grad search enough. Let's implement this and drop coordinate for lower bug risk. We can keep coordinate? No need.

`_low_dim_search` with gradient:
- Generate start thetas as before.
- For each start, allocate time slice; call `_low_dim_grad_search` with deadline `now+time_per`.
- After each, update global best.
- Finally, use remaining time to continue grad search from global best theta (if stored). Need `_low_dim_grad_search` returns best theta. For each start, if it improves global, store its `best_theta`. At final, call again with that theta.
- Also if no starts, uniform.

Starts generation:
```
def _make_lowdim_starts(n, bases, seed):
    rng=...
    starts=[]
    # uniform theta zeros
    starts.append(np.zeros(D))
    # sine template thetas
    for amp in [0.5,0.8,-0.5,-0.8]:
       for phase in [0, np.pi/2, np.pi/4, 3*np.pi/4]:
         p=_make_template_sine(n,amp,phase)

def _low_dim_search(n, budget_s, seed=1, template_seqs=None, max_mode=10):
    n=int(n)
    if budget_s <= 10 or n <= 0: return None, np.inf
    bases=_make_bases(n, max_mode=max_mode)
    D=bases.shape[0]
    # build starts
    starts=[]
    def? no. Use loops.
    # prepend template thetas if provided
    if template_seqs:
       for seq in template_seqs:
          p=_prepare_sequence(seq, add_floor=False)
          if p is not None and len(p)==n:
             th=_theta_from_sequence(p,bases)
             if th is not None: starts.append(th)
    # add uniform
    starts.append(np.zeros(D))
    # sine templates
    for amp in (0.5, 0.8, -0.5, -0.8):
       for phase in (0.0, 0.5*np.pi, 0.25*np.pi, 0.75*np.pi):
          p=_make_template_sine(n, amp, phase)
          th=_theta_from_sequence(p,bases)
          if th is not None: starts.append(th)
    # recurrence
    p=_recurrence_template(n)
    th=_theta_from_sequence(p,bases)
    if th is not None: starts.append(th)
    # random
    rng=np.random.default_rng(seed)
    for _ in range(3): starts.append(rng.normal(0,0.5,D))
    # limit 10
    starts=starts[:10]
    best_p=None; best_score=inf; best_theta=None
    start_t=time.monotonic()
    # allocate per start, leaving 30% for final? We can just loop while time < budget - 2*per? simpler:
    per = max(8.0, (budget_s*0.7)/len(starts))
    for th in starts:
       if time.monotonic() - start_t >= budget_s - 2.0: break
       dt = per
       th_out, sc_out, p_out, theta_out = _low_dim_grad_search(th, bases, time.monotonic()+dt, seed=int(seed+hash? no hash) )
```

def _best_candidate(cands):

def _sort_candidates(cands):
    out=[]
    for i in range(len(cands)):
       name,p,score=cands[i]
       if p is None: continue
       if not np.isfinite(score): continue
       out.append((float(score), i, name, p))
    out.sort()
    return out
```

Function:
```
def _singular_template(n, kind='left'):
  i=np.arange(n)+0.5
  if kind=='left': y=1/np.sqrt(i)
  elif 'right': y=1/np.sqrt(n-i)
  elif 'both': y=1/np.sqrt(i)+1/np.sqrt(n-i)
  normalize
```

Let's add singular templates:
```
def _singular_template(n, power=0.5, side='left'):
   i=np.arange(n)+0.5
   if side=='left': y=np.power(i, -power)
   elif side=='right': y=np.power(n-i, -power)
   elif side=='both': y=np.power(i,-power)+np.power(n-i,-power)
   elif side=='center': y=1.0
   # maybe add small uniform floor for positivity? Values positive.
```

Detailed `_local_search` code:
```
def _local_search(p0, budget_s, seed=0, tag=''):
    if budget_s is None: return None,np.inf
    budget_s=float(budget_s)
    p=_prepare_sequence(p0, add_floor=True)
    if p is None: return None,np.inf
    n=p.size
    c=_fft_convolve(p)
    score=_score_from_conv(n,c,1.0)
    if not np.isfinite(score): return p, score
    best=p.copy(); best_score=score
    rng=np.random.default_rng(seed)
    t0=time.monotonic(); t_end=t0 + max(0.0, budget_s - 5.0)
    lr=0.05; stall=0; it=0
    while time.monotonic() < t_end:
       elapsed=time.monotonic()-t0
       frac=elapsed / max(1e-9, budget_s)
       if frac < 0.25:
          K=min(64, max(16, n//64 + 16)); beta=5.0
       elif frac < 0.6:
          K=min(96, max(24, n//32 + 8)); beta=10.0
       elif frac < 0.85:
          K=32; beta=40.0
       else:
          K=16; beta=120.0
       grad=_active_gradient(p,c,K,beta)
       if not np.all(np.isfinite(grad)):
          grad=np.zeros(n,dtype=np.float64)
       if np.linalg.norm(grad) < 1e-18:
          # perturb
          p=best.copy(); c=_fft_convolve(p); score=_score_from_conv(n,c,1.0)
          noise=rng.normal(0.0,0.05,n); ...
          stall+=1
          continue
       q=_mirror_update(p,grad,lr)
       c2=_fft_convolve(q); sc2=...
       accepted=False
       if sc2 < score:
          p=q; c=c2; score=sc2; accepted=True
          lr=min(1.5, lr*1.08)
          if sc2 < best_score:
             best=p.copy(); best_score=sc2
       else:
          if lr > 0.003:
             q2=_mirror_update(p,grad,lr*0.5); c2b=...; scb=...
             if scb < score:
                p=q2; c=c2b; score=scb; lr *=0.5; accepted=True
                if scb < best_score: best=...
          if not accepted:
             lr=max(0.002, lr*0.8); stall+=1
       if accepted: stall=0
       else: ...
       if stall > 150:
          ... reset perturb
       it +=1
       if tag and it % 200 ==0:
          print(f"[{tag}] it={it} score={score:.8f} best={best_score:.8f}")
    return best, best_score
```
In accepted branch, if improves but not best, stall=0. Good.

```python
"""
Search for non-negative step-function heights minimizing
    score = 2*n*max_k((a*a)_k)/(sum(a))^2.

Strategy:
  * Work in the scale-invariant normalized form sum(a)=1.
  * Use fast FFT convolution internally, and evaluate_sequence only for final
    validation of a few candidates.
  * Strong starting shapes:
      - asymmetric i^{-1/2}-type boundary shapes,
      - truncated formal square roots of a flat convolution target,
      - low-dimensional positive Fourier + polynomial parametrization optimized
        by Adam-like soft-active-set gradient search.
  * Full-dimensional local refinement on the simplex using mirror updates in
    the direction of a soft active-set gradient of the largest convolution
    coefficients.
  * Progressive length refinement (1024 -> 2048 -> 4096 -> optionally 8192),
    with time guards and multiple seeds.
"""

import time
import numpy as np

_EPS = 1e-12


# ----------------------------------------------------------------------
# Basic utilities
# ----------------------------------------------------------------------

def _next_pow2(m):
    n = 1
    while n < m:
        n <<= 1
    return n


def _fft_convolve(a):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    n = a.size
    if n == 0:
        return np.empty(0, dtype=np.float64)
    need = 2 * n - 1
    N = _next_pow2(need)
    fa = np.fft.rfft(a, N)
    c = np.fft.irfft(fa * fa, N)
    c = c[:need]
    c = np.where(np.isfinite(c), c, 0.0)
    return c


def _score_from_conv(n, c, sum_a=1.0):
    if c.size == 0:
        return np.inf
    mx = float(np.max(c))
    if not np.isfinite(mx):
        return np.inf
    if mx < 0.0:
        mx = 0.0
    s = float(sum_a)
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s < 0.01:
        return np.inf
    return float(2.0 * n * mx / (s * s))


def _prepare_sequence(x, add_floor=True):
    if x is None:
        return None
    try:
        arr = np.asarray(x, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    except Exception:
        return None
    if arr.size == 0:
        return None
    arr = np.where(np.isfinite(arr), arr, 0.0)
    arr = np.clip(arr, 0.0, 1000.0)
    s = float(arr.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s < 0.01:
        return np.full(arr.size, 1.0 / float(arr.size), dtype=np.float64)
    arr = arr / s
    if add_floor:
        floor = 1e-12 / float(arr.size)
        arr = np.maximum(arr, floor)
        s2 = float(arr.sum())
        if np.isfinite(s2) and s2 > 0.0:
            arr = arr / s2
    return arr


def _internal_score(x):
    p = _prepare_sequence(x, add_floor=False)
    if p is None:
        return np.inf
    c = _fft_convolve(p)
    return _score_from_conv(p.size, c, float(p.sum()))


def _score_and_conv(x):
    p = _prepare_sequence(x, add_floor=False)
    if p is None:
        return None, None, np.inf
    c = _fft_convolve(p)
    sc = _score_from_conv(p.size, c, float(p.sum()))
    return p, c, sc


def _resample_seq(p, n_new):
    p = _prepare_sequence(p, add_floor=False)
    n_new = int(n_new)
    if p is None or n_new <= 0:
        return None
    if n_new == p.size:
        return p.copy()
    x_old = (np.arange(p.size, dtype=np.float64) + 0.5) / float(p.size)
    x_new = (np.arange(n_new, dtype=np.float64) + 0.5) / float(n_new)
    q = np.interp(x_new, x_old, p)
    q = np.where(np.isfinite(q), q, 0.0)
    q = np.maximum(q, 0.0)
    s = float(q.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s < 1e-12:
        q = np.full(n_new, 1.0 / float(n_new), dtype=np.float64)
    else:
        q = q / s
    return q


# ----------------------------------------------------------------------
# Candidate constructors
# ----------------------------------------------------------------------

def _template_uniform(n):
    n = int(n)
    if n <= 0:
        return None
    return np.full(n, 1.0 / float(n), dtype=np.float64)


def _template_sine(n, amp, phase=0.0, mode=1):
    n = int(n)
    if n <= 0:
        return None
    x = (np.arange(n, dtype=np.float64) + 0.5) / float(n)
    ang = 2.0 * np.pi * float(mode) * x + float(phase)
    y = 1.0 + float(amp) * np.sin(ang)
    y = np.maximum(y, 1e-6)
    y = np.where(np.isfinite(y), y, 1.0)
    s = float(y.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s < 1e-12:
        return _template_uniform(n)
    return y / s


def _singular_template(n, power=0.5, side="left"):
    n = int(n)
    if n <= 0:
        return None
    i = np.arange(n, dtype=np.float64) + 0.5
    j = float(n) - i
    if side == "left":
        y = np.power(i, -float(power))
    elif side == "right":
        y = np.power(j, -float(power))
    elif side == "both":
        y = np.power(i, -float(power)) + np.power(j, -float(power))
    else:
        y = np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64)
    y = np.where(np.isfinite(y), y, 0.0)
    y = np.maximum(y, 0.0)
    s = float(y.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s < 1e-12:
        return _template_uniform(n)
    return y / s


def _recurrence_template(n, shift=0):
    """
    Formal square-root-style initialization: solve p*p_k ~= 1/(2n-1) for
    the first n lags, then normalize.  This yields an i^{-1/2}-type profile
    and is usually close to the harmonic-limit quasi-optimal shape.
    """
    n = int(n)
    if n <= 0:
        return None
    L = 2 * n - 1
    p = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
    p[0] = 1.0 / np.sqrt(float(L))
    qbase = 1.0 / float(L)
    denom0 = 2.0 * p[0]
    for k in range(1, n):
        if k > 1:
            left = p[1:k]
            right = p[k - 1:0:-1]
            s = float(np.dot(left, right))
        else:
            s = 0.0
        val = (qbase - s) / denom0
        if not np.isfinite(val) or val < 0.0:
            val = 0.0
        p[k] = val

    if shift > 0:
        shift = int(min(shift, n - 1))
        out = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
        if n - shift > 0:
            out[shift:] = p[:n - shift]
        p = out

    return _prepare_sequence(p, add_floor=True)


# ----------------------------------------------------------------------
# Low-dimensional smooth parametrization search
# ----------------------------------------------------------------------

def _make_bases(n, max_mode=12):
    n = int(n)
    if n <= 1:
        return np.zeros((1, n), dtype=np.float64)
    x = (np.arange(n, dtype=np.float64) + 0.5) / float(n)
    parts = []
    for m in range(1, int(max_mode) + 1):
        ang = 2.0 * np.pi * float(m) * x
        parts.append(np.sin(ang))
        parts.append(np.cos(ang))
    xm = x - 0.5
    parts.append(xm)
    parts.append(xm * xm - 1.0 / 12.0)
    parts.append(xm * xm * xm)
    B = np.vstack(parts).astype(np.float64, copy=False)
    for r in range(B.shape[0]):
        row = B[r]
        row = row - row.mean()
        var = float(np.mean(row * row))
        if var > 1e-12:
            B[r] = row / np.sqrt(var)
    return B


def _sequence_from_theta(theta, bases):
    z = np.dot(bases, theta)
    z = np.where(np.isfinite(z), z, 0.0)
    z = np.clip(z, -5.0, 5.0)
    f = np.exp(z)
    f = np.where(f > 0.0, f, 1e-300)
    s = float(f.sum())
    n = bases.shape[1]
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s < 1e-12:
        return np.full(n, 1.0 / float(n), dtype=np.float64)
    return f / s


def _theta_from_sequence(seq, bases):
    p = _prepare_sequence(seq, add_floor=False)
    if p is None:
        return None
    try:
        y = np.log(np.maximum(p, 1e-12))
        y -= y.mean()
        theta, _, _, _ = np.linalg.lstsq(bases, y, rcond=None)
    except Exception:
        return None
    if theta is None:
        return None
    theta = np.where(np.isfinite(theta), theta, 0.0)
    return np.clip(theta, -6.0, 6.0)


def _theta_gradient(p, bases, center_grad):
    # p and center_grad are 1D length n.  bases is D x n.
    wg = center_grad * p
    term1 = np.dot(bases, wg)
    m = np.dot(bases, p)
    s = float(wg.sum())
    return term1 - m * s


def _active_gradient(p, c, K, beta):
    n = p.size
    K = int(min(max(K, 1), c.size))
    if c.size == 0:
        return np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
    if K < c.size:
        try:
            idx = np.argpartition(c, -K)[-K:]
        except Exception:
            idx = np.arange(c.size, dtype=np.int64)
    else:
        idx = np.arange(c.size, dtype=np.int64)
    vals = c[idx]
    cmax = float(np.max(vals)) if vals.size else 0.0
    if cmax <= 0.0 or beta <= 0.0:
        w = np.full(idx.size, 1.0 / float(idx.size), dtype=np.float64)
    else:
        z = beta * (vals - cmax)
        z = np.where(np.isfinite(z), z, 0.0)
        w = np.exp(z)
        ws = float(w.sum())
        if not np.isfinite(ws) or ws <= 0.0:
            w = np.full(idx.size, 1.0 / float(idx.size), dtype=np.float64)
        else:
            w = w / ws

    grad = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
    for k_int, ww in zip(idx, w):
        if ww <= 1e-16:
            continue
        k = int(k_int)
        l = k - (n - 1)
        if l < 0:
            l = 0
        r = k
        if r >= n:
            r = n - 1
        if r < l:
            continue
        seg = p[k - r:k - l + 1][::-1]
        grad[l:r + 1] += (2.0 * ww) * seg

    grad -= grad.mean()
    grad = np.where(np.isfinite(grad), grad, 0.0)
    return grad


def _mirror_update(p, grad, lr):
    n = p.size
    if grad.size != n or n == 0:
        return p.copy()
    if not np.any(np.abs(grad) > 1e-18):
        return p.copy()
    g = grad - grad.mean()
    g = g * float(n)
    exponent = -float(lr) * g
    exponent = np.where(np.isfinite(exponent), exponent, 0.0)
    exponent = np.clip(exponent, -5.0, 5.0)
    q = p * np.exp(exponent)
    q = np.where(np.isfinite(q), q, 0.0)
    q = np.maximum(q, 0.0)
    s = float(q.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 0.0:
        return p.copy()
    q = q / s
    return q


def _perturb_sequence(p, rng, temp=0.05):
    n = p.size
    noise = rng.normal(0.0, float(temp), size=n)
    noise = np.clip(noise, -3.0, 3.0)
    q = p * np.exp(noise)
    q = np.where(np.isfinite(q), q, 0.0)
    q = np.maximum(q, 0.0)
    s = float(q.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 0.0:
        return p.copy()
    return q / s


def _low_dim_grad_search(theta, bases, deadline, seed=0):
    theta = np.asarray(theta, dtype=np.float64).copy()
    D = theta.size
    p = _sequence_from_theta(theta, bases)
    c = _fft_convolve(p)
    n = p.size
    score = _score_from_conv(n, c, 1.0)
    best_theta = theta.copy()
    best_p = p.copy()
    best_score = score

    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)
    m1 = np.zeros(D, dtype=np.float64)
    v1 = np.zeros(D, dtype=np.float64)
    step = 0.1
    stall = 0
    t0 = time.monotonic()

    while time.monotonic() < deadline:
        frac = (time.monotonic() - t0) / max(1e-9, max(1e-9, deadline - t0))
        if frac < 0.3:
            K = min(48, max(12, n // 64 + 12))
            beta = 8.0
        elif frac < 0.65:
            K = min(64, max(24, n // 32 + 12))
            beta = 16.0
        else:
            K = min(32, max(12, n // 64 + 12))
            beta = 60.0

        gradp = _active_gradient(p, c, K, beta)
        gth = _theta_gradient(p, bases, gradp)
        gth = np.where(np.isfinite(gth), gth, 0.0)

        m1 = 0.9 * m1 + 0.1 * gth
        v1 = 0.99 * v1 + 0.01 * (gth * gth)
        upd = m1 / (np.sqrt(v1) + 1e-8)
        upd = np.where(np.isfinite(upd), upd, 0.0)
        nrm = float(np.linalg.norm(upd))
        if not np.isfinite(nrm) or nrm < 1e-12:
            stall += 1
            step = max(0.02, step * 0.7)
            if stall > 60:
                theta = best_theta + rng.normal(0.0, 0.25, size=D)
                theta = np.clip(theta, -6.0, 6.0)
                p = _sequence_from_theta(theta, bases)
                c = _fft_convolve(p)
                score = _score_from_conv(n, c, 1.0)
                stall = 0
                step = 0.1
            continue
        upd = upd / nrm

        accepted = False
        trial_steps = (step, min(2.0, step * 2.0), max(0.02, step * 0.5))
        for ts in trial_steps:
            th_try = np.clip(theta - float(ts) * upd, -6.0, 6.0)
            p_try = _sequence_from_theta(th_try, bases)
            c_try = _fft_convolve(p_try)
            sc_try = _score_from_conv(n, c_try, 1.0)
            if np.isfinite(sc_try) and sc_try < score:
                theta = th_try
                p = p_try
                c = c_try
                score = sc_try
                step = min(1.5, step * 1.12)
                accepted = True
                if sc_try < best_score:
                    best_score = sc_try
                    best_p = p.copy()
                    best_theta = theta.copy()
                break

        if not accepted:
            step = max(0.02, step * 0.7)
            stall += 1
            if stall > 70:
                theta = best_theta.copy() + rng.normal(0.0, 0.25, size=D)
                theta = np.clip(theta, -6.0, 6.0)
                p = _sequence_from_theta(theta, bases)
                c = _fft_convolve(p)
                score = _score_from_conv(n, c, 1.0)
                if score > best_score * 1.2:
                    theta = best_theta.copy()
                    p = best_p.copy()
                    c = _fft_convolve(p)
                    score = best_score
                stall = 0
                step = 0.1

        if time.monotonic() >= deadline:
            break

    return best_theta, best_score, best_p


def _low_dim_search(n, budget_s, seed=1, template_seqs=None, max_mode=12):
    n = int(n)
    if n <= 0 or budget_s <= 15.0:
        return None, np.inf

    budget_s = float(budget_s)
    bases = _make_bases(n, max_mode=max_mode)
    D = int(bases.shape[0])

    starts = []
    rng_all = np.random.default_rng(seed)

    if template_seqs is not None:
        for seq in template_seqs:
            p = _prepare_sequence(seq, add_floor=False)
            if p is not None and p.size == n:
                th = _theta_from_sequence(p, bases)
                if th is not None:
                    starts.append(th)

    starts.append(np.zeros(D, dtype=np.float64))

    for pw in (0.45, 0.5, 0.55):
        for side in ("left", "right"):
            p = _singular_template(n, power=pw, side=side)
            th = _theta_from_sequence(p, bases)
            if th is not None:
                starts.append(th)

    p = _recurrence_template(n)
    th = _theta_from_sequence(p, bases)
    if th is not None:
        starts.append(th)

    for amp in (0.5, 0.8, -0.5, -0.8):
        for phase in (0.0, 0.5 * np.pi, 0.25 * np.pi, 0.75 * np.pi):
            p = _template_sine(n, amp, phase)
            th = _theta_from_sequence(p, bases)
            if th is not None:
                starts.append(th)

    for _ in range(3):
        starts.append(rng_all.normal(0.0, 0.5, size=D))

    starts = starts[:10]

    best_p = None
    best_score = np.inf
    best_theta = None
    start_t = time.monotonic()
    per_start = max(6.0, (budget_s * 0.72) / max(1, len(starts)))

    for idx, th0 in enumerate(starts):
        if time.monotonic() - start_t >= budget_s - 10.0:
            break
        deadline = min(start_t + budget_s - 10.0, time.monotonic() + per_start)
        th_out, sc_out, p_out = _low_dim_grad_search(th0, bases, deadline, seed=int(seed + 100 * idx))
        if p_out is not None and np.isfinite(sc_out):
            if sc_out < best_score:
                best_score = float(sc_out)
                best_p = p_out.copy()
                best_theta = th_out.copy()

    remaining = budget_s - (time.monotonic() - start_t)
    if best_p is not None and remaining > 20.0 and best_theta is not None:
        deadline = time.monotonic() + max(0.0, remaining - 5.0)
        th_out, sc_out, p_out = _low_dim_grad_search(best_theta, bases, deadline, seed=int(seed + 999))
        if p_out is not None and np.isfinite(sc_out) and sc_out < best_score:
            best_score = float(sc_out)
            best_p = p_out.copy()

    if best_p is None:
        return _template_uniform(n), _internal_score(_template_uniform(n))
    return best_p, best_score


# ----------------------------------------------------------------------
# Full-dimensional local search
# ----------------------------------------------------------------------

def _local_search(p0, budget_s, seed=0, tag=""):
    p = _prepare_sequence(p0, add_floor=True)
    if p is None:
        return None, np.inf
    budget_s = float(budget_s)
    if budget_s <= 1.0:
        sc = _internal_score(p)
        return p, sc

    n = p.size
    c = _fft_convolve(p)
    score = _score_from_conv(n, c, 1.0)
    if not np.isfinite(score):
        return p, score

    best_p = p.copy()
    best_score = score
    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)
    t0 = time.monotonic()
    t_end = t0 + max(0.0, budget_s - 5.0)
    lr = 0.05
    stall = 0
    it = 0

    while time.monotonic() < t_end:
        elapsed = time.monotonic() - t0
        frac = elapsed / max(1e-9, budget_s)

        if frac < 0.25:
            K = min(64, max(16, n // 64 + 16))
            beta = 5.0
        elif frac < 0.60:
            K = min(96, max(24, n // 32 + 8))
            beta = 12.0
        elif frac < 0.85:
            K = 32
            beta = 45.0
        else:
            K = 16
            beta = 140.0

        grad = _active_gradient(p, c, K, beta)
        if not np.all(np.isfinite(grad)) or float(np.linalg.norm(grad)) < 1e-18:
            stall += 1
            if stall > 20:
                p = best_p.copy()
                c = _fft_convolve(p)
                score = _score_from_conv(n, c, 1.0)
                perp = _perturb_sequence(p, rng, 0.05)
                cp = _fft_convolve(perp)
                scp = _score_from_conv(n, cp, 1.0)
                if np.isfinite(scp) and scp < score * 1.25:
                    p = perp
                    c = cp
                    score = scp
                lr = 0.05
                stall = 0
            continue

        q = _mirror_update(p, grad, lr)
        c2 = _fft_convolve(q)
        sc2 = _score_from_conv(n, c2, 1.0)
        accepted = False

        if np.isfinite(sc2) and sc2 < score:
            p = q
            c = c2
            score = sc2
            lr = min(1.5, lr * 1.08)
            accepted = True
            if sc2 < best_score:
                best_score = float(sc2)
                best_p = p.copy()
        else:
            if lr > 0.003:
                q2 = _mirror_update(p, grad, lr * 0.5)
                c2b = _fft_convolve(q2)
                scb = _score_from_conv(n, c2b, 1.0)
                if np.isfinite(scb) and scb < score:
                    p = q2
                    c = c2b
                    score = scb
                    lr *= 0.5
                    accepted = True
                    if scb < best_score:
                        best_score = float(scb)
                        best_p = p.copy()
            if not accepted:
                lr = max(0.002, lr * 0.8)
                stall += 1

        if accepted:
            stall = 0
        else:
            stall += 1

        if stall > 160:
            p = best_p.copy()
            c = _fft_convolve(p)
            score = _score_from_conv(n, c, 1.0)
            perp = _perturb_sequence(p, rng, 0.05)
            cp = _fft_convolve(perp)
            scp = _score_from_conv(n, cp, 1.0)
            if np.isfinite(scp) and scp < score * 1.25:
                p = perp
                c = cp
                score = scp
            lr = 0.05
            stall = 0

        it += 1
        if tag and (it % 250 == 0):
            print(f"[{tag}] it={it} score={score:.8f} best={best_score:.8f} n={n}")

    return best_p, best_score


# ----------------------------------------------------------------------
# Candidate bookkeeping
# ----------------------------------------------------------------------

def _add_candidate(cands, name, p, score=None):
    if p is None:
        return
    p = _prepare_sequence(p, add_floor=False)
    if p is None or p.size == 0:
        return
    if score is None or not np.isfinite(score):
        score = _internal_score(p)
    if not np.isfinite(score):
        return
    cands.append((str(name), p, float(score)))


def _sort_candidates(cands):
    arr = []
    for idx, item in enumerate(cands):
        if len(item) != 3:
            continue
        name, p, score = item
        if p is None or not np.isfinite(score):
            continue
        arr.append((float(score), idx, str(name), p))
    arr.sort()
    return [(name, p, score) for score, _idx, name, p in arr]


def _best_candidate(cands):
    best_name = "none"
    best_p = None
    best_score = np.inf
    for name, p, score in cands:
        if p is None or not np.isfinite(score):
            continue
        if score < best_score:
            best_score = float(score)
            best_p = p.copy()
            best_name = str(name)
    return best_name, best_p, best_score


# ----------------------------------------------------------------------
# Main entry point
# ----------------------------------------------------------------------

def propose_candidate(seed=42, budget_s=2400, **kwargs):
    t0 = time.monotonic()
    total = max(60.0, float(budget_s))
    hard_deadline = t0 + total - 20.0
    candidates = []
    seed = int(seed)

    # Inherited / previous best sequence, if available.
    global_seq = globals().get("height_sequence_1", None)
    _add_candidate(candidates, "global", global_seq)

    # Reliable deterministic base candidates.
    _add_candidate(candidates, "sing_left_half", _singular_template(2048, 0.5, "left"))
    _add_candidate(candidates, "sing_left_045", _singular_template(2048, 0.45, "left"))
    _add_candidate(candidates, "sing_left_055", _singular_template(2048, 0.55, "left"))
    _add_candidate(candidates, "rec2048", _recurrence_template(2048))
    _add_candidate(candidates, "rec2048_shift1", _recurrence_template(2048, 1))
    _add_candidate(candidates, "sine_0_5_pi", _template_sine(2048, 0.5, 0.5 * np.pi))
    _add_candidate(candidates, "sine_-0_5_pi", _template_sine(2048, -0.5, 0.5 * np.pi))
    _add_candidate(candidates, "sine_0_8_0", _template_sine(2048, 0.8, 0.0))

    # Stage 1: low-dimensional search at 1024.
    if hard_deadline - time.monotonic() > 1100.0:
        low_b = min(160.0, hard_deadline - time.monotonic() - 1400.0)
        if low_b > 50.0:
            p1024, s1024 = _low_dim_search(1024, low_b, seed=seed + 1)
            _add_candidate(candidates, "lowdim1024", p1024, s1024)
            print(f"[search] lowdim1024 score={s1024:.8f}")

    # Stage 2: low-dimensional search at 2048, seeded by current best if possible.
    if hard_deadline - time.monotonic() > 1000.0:
        _, tmp_p, tmp_s = _best_candidate(candidates)
        templates = []
        if tmp_p is not None:
            up2048 = _resample_seq(tmp_p, 2048)
            if up2048 is not None:
                templates.append(up2048)
        low2_b = min(130.0, hard_deadline - time.monotonic() - 1200.0)
        if low2_b > 40.0:
            plow2, slow2 = _low_dim_search(2048, low2_b, seed=seed + 2, template_seqs=templates)
            _add_candidate(candidates, "lowdim2048", plow2, slow2)
            print(f"[search] lowdim2048 score={slow2:.8f}")

    # Stage 3: full-dimensional local refinement at 2048.
    rem = hard_deadline - time.monotonic()
    if rem > 700.0:
        _best_name, best_p, best_score = _best_candidate(candidates)
        stage0 = []
        if best_p is not None:
            _add_candidate(stage0, "best_up2048", _resample_seq(best_p, 2048))
        _add_candidate(stage0, "rec2048", _recurrence_template(2048))
        _add_candidate(stage0, "rec2048_shift1", _recurrence_template(2048, 1))
        _add_candidate(stage0, "sing_left_half2048", _singular_template(2048, 0.5, "left"))
        if global_seq is not None:
            _add_candidate(stage0, "global_up2048", _resample_seq(global_seq, 2048))

        stage_sorted = _sort_candidates(stage0)[:4]
        reserve = 900.0 if rem > 2200 else 600.0
        budget_stage = max(120.0, min(900.0, rem - reserve))
        per = max(35.0, budget_stage / max(1, len(stage_sorted)))
        for idx, (nm, pp, sc) in enumerate(stage_sorted):
            if hard_deadline - time.monotonic() < per + 80.0:
                break
            outp, outsc = _local_search(pp, per, seed=seed + 100 + idx, tag="n2048")
            _add_candidate(candidates, "ref2048_" + nm, outp, outsc)
            print(f"[search] ref2048_{nm} score={outsc:.8f}")

    # Stage 4: length increase to 4096 if worthwhile.
    rem = hard_deadline - time.monotonic()
    if rem > 900.0:
        _, best_p, best_score = _best_candidate(candidates)
        stage0 = []
        if best_p is not None:
            _add_candidate(stage0, "best_up4096", _resample_seq(best_p, 4096))
        _add_candidate(stage0, "rec4096", _recurrence_template(4096))
        _add_candidate(stage0, "sing_left_half4096", _singular_template(4096, 0.5, "left"))
        stage_sorted = _sort_candidates(stage0)[:3]
        reserve = 500.0 if rem > 1500 else 250.0
        budget_stage = max(120.0, min(700.0, rem - reserve))
        per = max(35.0, budget_stage / max(1, len(stage_sorted)))
        for idx, (nm, pp, sc) in enumerate(stage_sorted):
            if hard_deadline - time.monotonic() < per + 120.0:
                break
            outp, outsc = _local_search(pp, per, seed=seed + 200 + idx, tag="n4096")
            _add_candidate(candidates, "ref4096_" + nm, outp, outsc)
            print(f"[search] ref4096_{nm} score={outsc:.8f}")

    # Stage 5: optional 8192 if much time remains and improvement likely.
    rem = hard_deadline - time.monotonic()
    if rem > 1200.0:
        _, best_p, best_score = _best_candidate(candidates)
        if best_p is not None and best_score > 1.48:
            stage0 = []
            _add_candidate(stage0, "best_up8192", _resample_seq(best_p, 8192))
            stage0 = _sort_candidates(stage0)[:1]
            per = min(450.0, rem - 350.0)
            if per > 120.0:
                outp, outsc = _local_search(stage0[0][1], per, seed=seed + 300, tag="n8192")
                _add_candidate(candidates, "ref8192_best", outp, outsc)
                print(f"[search] ref8192_best score={outsc:.8f}")

    # Final polish on the current best candidate.
    rem = hard_deadline - time.monotonic()
    if rem > 60.0:
        _, best_p, best_score = _best_candidate(candidates)
        if best_p is not None:
            per = max(30.0, min(320.0, rem - 25.0))
            outp, outsc = _local_search(best_p, per, seed=seed + 500, tag="finalPolish")
            _add_candidate(candidates, "finalPolish", outp, outsc)
            print(f"[search] finalPolish score={outsc:.8f}")

    # Validate top candidates with the exact evaluator.
    ordered = _sort_candidates(candidates)
    best_actual = np.inf
    best_out = None

    for idx, (nm, pp, sc) in enumerate(ordered[:4]):
        if time.monotonic() > hard_deadline:
            break
        if pp is None:
            continue
        arr = _prepare_sequence(pp, add_floor=False)
        if arr is None or arr.size == 0 or arr.size > 16384:
            continue
        try:
            real = float(evaluate_sequence(arr.tolist()))
        except Exception:
            real = np.inf
        if np.isfinite(real) and real < best_actual:
            best_actual = real
            best_out = arr
            print(f"[validate] {nm} n={arr.size} actual={real:.8f} internal={sc:.8f}")
            if real < 1.45:
                break

    if best_out is None and ordered:
        # Return the best internally scored sanitized sequence if evaluation
        # did not finish or was invalid.
        _, pp, _sc = ordered[0]
        best_out = _prepare_sequence(pp, add_floor=False)

    if best_out is None:
        # Very safe fallback.
        best_out = np.full(1024, 1.0 / 1024.0, dtype=np.float64)

    out = np.asarray(best_out, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    out = np.where(np.isfinite(out), out, 0.0)
    out = np.clip(out, 0.0, 1000.0)
    sout = float(out.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(sout) or sout < 0.01:
        out = np.full(1024, 1.0 / 1024.0, dtype=np.float64)
    else:
        out = out / sout

    return [float(x) for x in out]
```
